# Azure CIS adversarial verification

Changed 43 requirement decisions or mappings. All 2,068 rows were checked by the validation script for identity, catalogue references, and summary consistency.

## Changes

- cis_2.0_azure / 2.1.16: MAPPED azure.claude.defender_assessment_vulnerability_solution → NEW_CHECK azure.cis.auto_provision_vulnerability_assessment_machines. The mapped evaluator tests assessment health or plan pricing, not auto provisioning of the named component.
- cis_2.0_azure / 2.1.17: MAPPING_FIX azure.port.securitycenter_azure_defender_on_for_containers → NEW_CHECK azure.cis.auto_provision_defender_containers_components. The mapped evaluator tests assessment health or plan pricing, not auto provisioning of the named component.
- cis_2.1_azure / 2.1.15: MAPPED azure.claude.defender_assessment_vulnerability_solution → NEW_CHECK azure.cis.auto_provision_vulnerability_assessment_machines. The mapped evaluator tests assessment health or plan pricing, not auto provisioning of the named component.
- cis_2.1_azure / 2.1.16: MAPPING_FIX azure.port.securitycenter_azure_defender_on_for_containers → NEW_CHECK azure.cis.auto_provision_defender_containers_components. The mapped evaluator tests assessment health or plan pricing, not auto provisioning of the named component.
- cis_4.0_azure / 6.2.2: PARTIAL azure.claude.entra_trusted_named_location_exists → PARTIAL azure.cis.exclusionary_geographic_access_policy_considered. A named location alone does not show that geographic policy was considered or enforced. The policy signal is automatable; consideration needs review evidence.
- cis_4.0_azure / 6.3.2: PARTIAL azure.claude.entra_guest_access_restricted → NEW_CHECK azure.cis.guest_users_reviewed_regular_basis. Guest access restriction tests guest permissions, not recurring review completion; use access review definitions and instances.
- cis_4.0_azure / 7.1.1.8: MAPPING_FIX azure.claude.subscription_diagnostic_setting_exists → NEW_CHECK azure.cis.entra_graph_activity_diagnostic. The subscription_diagnostic_setting_exists evaluator reads subscription activity log settings, not Microsoft Entra tenant logs.
- cis_4.0_azure / 7.1.1.9: MAPPING_FIX azure.claude.subscription_diagnostic_setting_exists → NEW_CHECK azure.cis.entra_activity_diagnostic. The subscription_diagnostic_setting_exists evaluator reads subscription activity log settings, not Microsoft Entra tenant logs.
- cis_4.0_azure / 10.3.11: NEW_CHECK azure.cis.resource_manager_readonly_locks_considered_storage_accounts → MANUAL (none). A read-only lock inventory shows configuration, but the requirement asks whether applying ReadOnly locks was considered; approval or exception evidence is needed.
- cis_5.0_azure / 2.1.9: MAPPED azure.claude.databricks_vnet_injection_value → MAPPING_FIX azure.port.databricks_workspace_no_public_ip_enabled. VNet injection does not establish the no-public-IP workspace flag; the dedicated Databricks evaluator checks that flag.
- cis_5.0_azure / 5.2.2: PARTIAL azure.claude.entra_trusted_named_location_exists → PARTIAL azure.cis.exclusionary_geographic_access_policy_considered. A named location alone does not show that geographic policy was considered or enforced. The policy signal is automatable; consideration needs review evidence.
- cis_5.0_azure / 5.3.2: PARTIAL azure.claude.entra_guest_access_restricted → NEW_CHECK azure.cis.guest_users_reviewed_regular_basis. Guest access restriction tests guest permissions, not recurring review completion; use access review definitions and instances.
- cis_5.0_azure / 6.1.1.8: MAPPED azure.claude.subscription_diagnostic_setting_exists → NEW_CHECK azure.cis.entra_graph_activity_diagnostic. The subscription_diagnostic_setting_exists evaluator reads subscription activity log settings, not Microsoft Entra tenant logs.

## Spot checks and limitations

- `peaso/checks/claude_azure_identity.py:281`: Security Defaults reads `isEnabled`; the prior ledger reason named unrelated guest-policy fields. Corrected evidence text in all mapped rows.
- `peaso/checks/claude_azure_network.py:371`: subscription diagnostic settings concern subscription activity logs; Microsoft Entra tenant logs require their own category and destination tests.
- `peaso/checks/claude_azure_identity.py:300`: guest access restriction is an authorization policy, not a recurring review.
- The proposed Entra SSPR and legacy MFA policy endpoints in remaining specs are unverified; these must not count as implemented or passing checks.
- Automated catalogue and source-location checks do not prove every evaluator predicate semantically matches every control. The retained mappings and proposed API operations need implementation review before manifest promotion.

These edits refine specs without changing requirement decisions or mappings.

## Repair pass

Reviewed every requirement in all 14 Azure CIS framework versions against the frozen input text, check-index titles and evaluator locations, and the independent spot-check. Repeated control families were corrected across versions. Proposed IDs from `new-checks-azure.json` are valid ledger references when paired with a local `new_checks` spec; their absence from `check-index.json` is not a defect.

### Repair findings

- Downgraded NSG RDP, SSH, HTTP(S), and UDP mappings where a port or network-rule predicate omitted trusted-source decisions, alternate ingress paths, or periodic review. Replaced the selected-UDP-port evaluator with the any-UDP evaluator where the requirement covers UDP generally.
- Corrected storage key rotation, blob-and-container soft delete, subnet NSG association, subscription diagnostic categories, and storage private-link mappings to evaluators that inspect the named condition.
- Downgraded subscription-level existence checks for NSG/VNet flow logs and Application Insights where they cannot prove every applicable resource is configured. Downgraded critical-resource and latest-TLS claims when classification or current-version evidence is absent.
- Removed proposed checks with undefined policy inventories, guessed tenant setting keys, or unsupported runtime catalogues; the affected rows now require evidence specific to the setting. For broad requirements with a concrete API signal, PARTIAL reasons state the remaining scope or process evidence.
- Revised retained proposals to name resource kinds, read-only endpoints or collected fields, and explicit PASS/FAIL predicates. Consolidated the monitoring-agent auto-provisioning proposal under `azure.cis.auto_provisioning_log_analytics_agent_vms` and the duplicate PostgreSQL single-server logging proposals under their canonical IDs; each reused proposal spec is identical.
- The three sampled Azure CIS entries in `spotcheck.md` were re-evaluated. `cis_5.0_azure/8.1.3.5` and `cis_3.0_azure/8.9` retain proposed IDs with local specs; `cis_v500/2_1_3` is MANUAL because the proposed Databricks worker-encryption field is unverified. No proposed check was promoted to an implemented evaluator.

### Validation

- Matched all 2,068 ledger rows in order to the 14 frozen input frameworks. No missing or duplicate requirement IDs.
- Checked every `native_checks` and `removed_checks` reference against `check-index.json` or a retained proposed spec, and every proposed ID against `new-checks-azure.json`.
- Checked decision shapes, required spec keys, Azure provider, nonempty reasons and fields, exact spec consistency for all 54 retained proposed IDs, summary counts, and 2,068 todo entries. All checks passed.
- Cross-checked EASM workspace, Defender server vulnerability-assessment setting, Defender pricing extension, and PostgreSQL private-endpoint APIs against Microsoft REST documentation: https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/rest/api/defenderforeasm/controlplanepreview/workspaces/list-by-subscription ; https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/rest/api/defenderforcloud-composite/server-vulnerability-assessments-settings/get ; https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/rest/api/defenderforcloud/pricings/update ; https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/rest/api/postgresql/private-endpoint-connections-part-1/list-by-server .

Unimplemented `new_checks` remain proposals and must not count as passing at runtime.

### Evaluator reread follow-up

A second framework-by-framework comparison removed loosely related runtime tags (including App Service managed identity for Entra registration and WDATP integration for endpoint component status), corrected log-container and notification fields, and narrowed claims for conditional-access, diagnostic-routing and resource-scope checks.

### Concrete App Service proposal

Nine App Service Entra registration rows now share `azure.cis.app_service_entra_registration_enabled`, which tests authSettingsV2 fields and a matching Microsoft Graph application rather than tagging managed-identity evaluators. This is a proposal only and is absent from native tags.

Repair-pass final counts: MAPPED=752, MAPPING_FIX=278, PARTIAL=537, MANUAL=260, NOT_APPLICABLE=0, NEW_CHECK=241.

Access-review proposals were made field-specific for role and guest scope queries, recurrence intervals and completed instance dates (28 references).

## Ledger repair 6

Reviewed 1,576 `MAPPED`, `MAPPING_FIX`, and `PARTIAL` requirements against 278 distinct native checks in `peaso/checks`. Replaced generated reasons that cited unrelated fields with the check-specific pass condition from the verified Azure check descriptions, cross-checked against the evaluator implementations.

### Counts

- Requirements reviewed: 1,576; distinct native checks: 278.
- Decision changes: MAPPED → MAPPING_FIX: 10, MAPPED → PARTIAL: 67, MAPPING_FIX → PARTIAL: 16.
- Reason-only changes: 862.
- Total requirements changed: 955.
- Final decisions: MAPPED=675, MAPPING_FIX=280, PARTIAL=621, MANUAL=260, NOT_APPLICABLE=0, NEW_CHECK=232.

### Every changed requirement

- `cis_2.0_azure` / `1.2.1`: reason: The mapped native evaluator reports azure.claude.entra_trusted_named_location_exists: PASS when an IP named location is trusted and declares a range.
- `cis_2.0_azure` / `1.3`: reason: The mapped native evaluator reports azure.claude.entra_users_cannot_create_tenants: PASS when `defaultUserRolePermissions.allowedToCreateTenants` is false.

### Validation

JSON parsing, per-framework requirement IDs and counts, native-check catalogue references, proposed-control references, and summary totals were checked after editing.
